docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design.md:6:`build_default_coordinator(config, acting=True, action_executor=...)` raises `ValueError` without an `action_executor` (pipeline_coordinator.py:1884). The CLI (`:1936`) only ever builds `acting=False` (shadow). The real executor "lands in a later phase" (ShadowActionExecutor docstring, :266) — it is unbuilt. This design builds it.
docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design.md:9:`JiraDispatchColdStartGateway` (pipeline_coordinator.py:606) is the LIVE gateway already used by the **cold-start** live path (`_default_cold_start_gateway(acting=True)`), and it already implements EVERY mutation the steady-state actions need:
docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design.md:12:So the steady-state §6 executor is a **thin dispatcher over the SAME proven live gateway** — not new mutation code. (`Action`: `pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:81`, frozen dataclass `kind`/`target`/`params`; `kind` ∈ `ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS`.)
docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design.md:30:- **D1 — error posture: fail-OPEN per-action.** A failed mutation logs + records `executed:False,error:...` and the tick CONTINUES (never crash the daemon / wedge the coordinator on one bad JIRA call). Rationale: the coordinator is the fleet's brain — one failed action must not take it down. (Mirrors the gateway's lazy/fail-open construction note, :608.)
docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design.md:33:- **D4 — env-gate, default-safe.** CLI `main()` reads `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_ACTING` (truthy → `acting=True` + `LiveActionExecutor`; default/unset → `acting=False` shadow). Code default stays acting=False. The flip is then a reversible env change + a coordinator restart — NOT a redeploy.
docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design.md:34:- **D5 — budget coupling.** Removing `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_DAILY_BUDGET_USD=0` happens AT the flip (restoring it while shadow pays for un-executed LLM calls). The env-gate doc must state both env changes go together.
docs/operations/2026-05-22-coordinator-acting-live-action-executor-design.md:35:- **D6 — decision-log audit.** Every executed action writes `executed:True` + outcome to the decision-log (the audit trail for "what did the autonomous coordinator actually do"). Already the daemon folds the executor result into the line.

docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:22:2. §2 — the contract surface: probe + gauge + flag + capability + UI.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:31:This spec is the **contract** — not the implementation. Code lands in `v2-⑨-1bc` (the `ai_core_probe` reference implementation + Prometheus gauge + in-process flag), `v2-⑨-2bc` (the `llm_fallback_chain` builder consumer + capability-inventory propagation), and `v2-⑨-Integration` (chaos test); operator-side hygiene for the current `ai_gateway` container lands in `v2-⑨-3a-Decision`. docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:45:| Available | `omnisight_aux_service_available{service="…"} == 1` | Service is **exposed**: included in capability inventory; included in fallback chains where applicable; UI shows the corresponding feature enabled; capability-aware code paths may delegate to it. | Capability inventory carries `capability:enable=<service>`; UI tile lit; Prom gauge `== 1`. |
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:46:| Unavailable | `omnisight_aux_service_available{service="…"} == 0` | Service is **quietly skipped**: removed from capability inventory; removed from fallback chains; UI hides the feature (no error toast, no degraded badge — the feature is simply not visible to the end-user); capability-aware code paths route around it. | Capability inventory does NOT carry `capability:enable=<service>`; UI tile not rendered; Prom gauge `== 0`; after 24 h continuous unavailability, a `severity=warn` alert fires (§4). |
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:60:- **NOT** a request-time fallback. The flag is consulted by *builders* (the `llm_fallback_chain` builder, the capability-inventory builder) — not by per-request hot paths. Per-request code reads the *already-built* chain. This keeps the hot path free of probe-state reads.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:64:The 2026-05-14 reframe (operator Q3) deliberately raised the abstraction layer from "ai-core hygiene" to "optional auxiliary service contract" precisely because the same pattern will be needed for omnisight-ai-engine (2026-Q3 plan) and omnisight-vision-core (2026-Q4 plan). The cost of writing the spec service-agnostic is near-zero (substitute `<service>` for `ai_core` in §2 and §3); the cost of *not* doing so is that each of the future sister services re-derives the pattern, drifts in details, and the cross-family invariants (alert severity, gauge name, capability propagation) silently disagree. §5 covers extensibility; §2 names "the auxiliary service" rather than "ai-core" throughout.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:105:- The gauge MUST NOT be exposed for services that are not registered (calling `.labels(service="…")` with an unregistered name is a programmer error and is rejected by §2.4 capability-inventory schema validation at builder time, not at probe time).
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:126:The capability inventory builder MUST consult `AUX_SERVICE_AVAILABLE` when deciding which `capability:enable=<service>` labels to expose. Specifically:
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:128:- The inventory builder runs at every relevant lifecycle moment (startup, settings reload, explicit `POST /admin/refresh-capabilities`, and once per probe cycle for the affected service).
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:129:- When `AUX_SERVICE_AVAILABLE[<name>] is True`, the inventory carries `capability:enable=<service>`.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:130:- When `AUX_SERVICE_AVAILABLE[<name>] is False`, the inventory **does not carry** that label — neither `capability:enable=<service>=false` nor `capability:disable=<service>`. The contract is *absence* not *negation*. This matches the existing `capability:enable=*` label convention from `docs/sop/jira-label-conventions.md` §"capability inventory".
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:131:- Downstream consumers of the capability inventory (the JIRA dispatcher's `capability:enable=*` matching for runner pickup, the UI's feature-tile renderer, the per-request RBAC layer's deferred-feature check) MUST treat absence as "feature unavailable" — no further check needed.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:146:The frontend reads the capability inventory (§2.4); rendering decisions cascade from there. No frontend code needs to know about probes, gauges, or flags directly — that boundary stays inside `backend/`.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:286:            seeing errors; capability-aware features are silently absent.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:327:- Alternative: the install's compose / deploy config sets `OMNISIGHT_AUX_SERVICE_DISABLE=ai_core,vision_core` (comma-separated). When a service is in this list, the probe is **not started** and no samples reach the gauge, so the 24 h-avg condition is never evaluated. The capability inventory simply never adds the corresponding `capability:enable=<service>` label, and the UI does not render the feature. This is the "we don't ship this auxiliary on this install" path.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:352:The same probe loop, the same gauge name (different label), the same in-process flag dict (different key), the same chain-builder-style consumer (specific to whatever the new service feeds — vision-core would feed a *vision capability chain*, not the LLM chain).
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:361:4. **Wire** the corresponding capability-inventory builder branch (e.g. `if VISION_CORE_AVAILABLE: caps.append("capability:enable=vision_core")`).
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:371:- **Cross-auxiliary dependencies.** If auxiliary A depends on auxiliary B (e.g. ai-engine needs ai-core), each probe is independent. The capability-inventory builder may encode "feature X requires both ai-core AND ai-engine" but the probe contract does not.
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:432:- The capability inventory (same reason).
docs/sprint-s12/2026-05-16-v2-family9-aux-service-contract.md:486:| `v2-⑨-2bc` | §2.4, §3.5 | `llm_fallback_chain` builder consumer + capability-inventory propagation |

docs/sop/lessons/L-OP-843-vendor-claim-disambiguate-before-deleting-resilience.md:12:**Situation**: Operator referenced a Lucas Gonzalez Pagliere talk at Code w/ Claude 2026 SF ("The expanding toolkit", 2026-05-06 11:15) claiming that capabilities which previously needed "heavy scaffolding" — reliable tool use, context management, code execution, computer use — "have moved into the model" so agents "finish work instead of just starting it". The implied reading: *we can delete a bunch of our retry / fault-tolerance code because Anthropic now handles it*.
docs/sop/lessons/L-OP-843-vendor-claim-disambiguate-before-deleting-resilience.md:41:* Independent audit (Augment Code 2026, "Anthropic Agent SDK: What It Ships vs. What It Leaves to You") cross-references each Anthropic claim against actual SDK surface. Confirms five-of-five resilience capabilities are caller-managed.
docs/sop/lessons/L-OP-843-vendor-claim-disambiguate-before-deleting-resilience.md:48:1. **Vendor talks compress capability layers**. Any pitch of the form "we now handle X so you don't have to" — including "no more retry boilerplate", "memory just works", "agents finish on their own" — is almost always conflating *at least* three distinct layers: transport (auto-retry HTTP), framework (agent loop / tool dispatch), and reliability (classification, breakers, resume, escalation, budget caps). The vendor genuinely ships some of these (usually the first two). The reliability layer almost always stays caller-managed because it is intrinsically deployment-specific. Don't delete reliability code on the strength of a single talk's framing.

    68	    {
    69	        ACTION_NOOP,
    70	        ACTION_FILE_TICKET,
    71	        ACTION_RELABEL,
    72	        ACTION_TRANSITION,

    75	        ACTION_ESCALATE,
    76	    }
    77	)

    80	@dataclass(frozen=True)
    81	class Action:
    82	    """A single thing the coordinator decides to do.
    83	
    84	    The skeleton only ever emits :class:`NoopAction`; the Tier-1 rule set
    85	    (29f-3) emits the §6.1 action kinds via the factory classmethods below.
    86	    The envelope is unchanged: ``kind`` is one of :data:`ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS`,
    87	    ``target`` is the JIRA key the action applies to, ``params`` carries the
    88	    kind-specific payload, and ``dry_run`` defaults to True so a half-wired
    89	    (or shadow-mode, 29f-14) daemon can never mutate JIRA/Gerrit.
    90	
    91	    "Union type" (Code AC): :class:`Action` + :class:`NoopAction` over the
    92	    constrained :data:`ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS` *is* the action union — a closed
    93	    set discriminated by ``kind`` rather than a ``typing.Union`` of N classes,
    94	    so the decision-log serialisation (:meth:`to_record`) stays uniform.
    95	    """
    96	
    97	    kind: str
    98	    target: str = ""
    99	    params: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
   100	    dry_run: bool = True
   101	
   102	    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
   103	        if not isinstance(self.kind, str) or not self.kind.strip():
   104	            raise ValueError("Action.kind must be a non-empty string")
   105	        if self.kind not in ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS:
   106	            raise ValueError(
   107	                f"Action.kind {self.kind!r} is not in the §6.1 allowed set "
   108	                f"{sorted(ALLOWED_ACTION_KINDS)}"
   109	            )
   110	        object.__setattr__(self, "params", dict(self.params))
   126	    @classmethod
   127	    def file_ticket(
   128	        cls,
   129	        *,
   130	        blocking: str,
   131	        target_area: str,
   132	        description: str,
   133	        dry_run: bool = True,
   134	    ) -> "Action":
   135	        """§6.1 file_ticket — file a dependency/work ticket in another area."""
   136	        return cls(
   137	            kind=ACTION_FILE_TICKET,
   138	            target=blocking,
   139	            params={
   140	                "blocking": blocking,
   141	                "target_area": target_area,
   142	                "description": description,
   143	            },
   144	            dry_run=dry_run,
   145	        )
   146	
   147	    @classmethod
   148	    def relabel(
   149	        cls,
   150	        ticket: str,
   151	        *,
   152	        add: tuple[str, ...] = (),
   153	        remove: tuple[str, ...] = (),
   154	        dry_run: bool = True,
   155	    ) -> "Action":
   156	        """§6.1 relabel — add/remove labels (never operator-only labels)."""
   157	        return cls(
   158	            kind=ACTION_RELABEL,
   159	            target=ticket,
   160	            params={"add": list(add), "remove": list(remove)},
   161	            dry_run=dry_run,
   162	        )
   163	
   164	    @classmethod
   165	    def transition(
   166	        cls, ticket: str, *, to_status: str, dry_run: bool = True
   167	    ) -> "Action":
   168	        """§6.1 transition — move a ticket through an allowed status edge."""
   169	        return cls(
   170	            kind=ACTION_TRANSITION,
   171	            target=ticket,
   172	            params={"to_status": to_status},
   173	            dry_run=dry_run,
   174	        )
   175	
   176	    @classmethod
   177	    def mention_operator(
   178	        cls,
   179	        ticket: str,
   180	        *,
   181	        message: str,
   182	        urgency: str = "medium",
   183	        dry_run: bool = True,
   184	    ) -> "Action":
   185	        """§6.1 mention_operator — @-mention operator; high urgency tags too."""
   186	        return cls(
   187	            kind=ACTION_MENTION_OPERATOR,
   188	            target=ticket,
   189	            params={"message": message, "urgency": urgency},
   190	            dry_run=dry_run,
   191	        )
   192	
   193	    @classmethod
   194	    def mark_for_followup(
   195	        cls, ticket: str, *, when: str, why: str, dry_run: bool = True
   196	    ) -> "Action":
   197	        """§6.1 mark_for_followup — defer attention via coord-resume-after."""
   198	        return cls(
   199	            kind=ACTION_MARK_FOR_FOLLOWUP,
   200	            target=ticket,
   201	            params={"when": when, "why": why},
   202	            dry_run=dry_run,
   203	        )
   204	
   205	    @classmethod
   206	    def escalate(cls, ticket: str, *, reason: str, dry_run: bool = True) -> "Action":
   207	        """§6.1 escalate — needs-operator-action label + reason comment."""
   208	        return cls(
   209	            kind=ACTION_ESCALATE,
   210	            target=ticket,
   211	            params={"reason": reason},
   212	            dry_run=dry_run,
   213	        )
   214	
   215	
   606	    can never be touched by a lower-priority rule (ADR §6.2 self-modify ban,
   607	    §6.3 operator override). Abstains (``None``) when there's no focal ticket
   608	    or the label is absent, so other rules still run.
   609	    """
   610	    t = ctx.ticket
   611	    if t is None or not has_label(t, COORD_SKIP_LABEL):
   612	        return None
   613	    return NoopAction()
   614	
   615	
   616	@rule(name="chain-deadlock", priority=5)
   617	def chain_deadlock(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   618	    """§5.1 #8 — A blocks B blocks A (or longer cycle): diagnose + escalate.
   619	
   620	    Walks the blockedBy DAG from the focal ticket over ``ctx.tickets``. If
   621	    the focal key is reachable from itself, that's a cycle — file a
   622	    diagnostic + @-mention operator (this should never happen; if it does,
   623	    halt rather than guess). Abstains when the graph is acyclic / unknown.
   624	    """
   625	    t = ctx.ticket
   626	    if t is None:
   627	        return None
   628	    # DFS over blockedBy edges looking for a path back to the focal key.
   629	    start = t.key
   630	    stack = list(t.blocked_by)
   631	    visited: set[str] = set()
   632	    while stack:
   633	        cur = stack.pop()
   634	        if cur == start:
   635	            return Action.mention_operator(
   636	                start,
   637	                message=(
   638	                    f"[chain-deadlock] blockedBy cycle detected through {start}; "
   639	                    f"halting automated routing — operator triage required."
   640	                ),
   641	                urgency="high",
   642	            )
   643	        if cur in visited:
   644	            continue
   645	        visited.add(cur)
   646	        nxt = ctx.tickets.get(cur)
   647	        if nxt is not None:
   648	            stack.extend(nxt.blocked_by)
   649	    return None
   650	
   651	
   652	@rule(name="revert-loop-quarantine", priority=10)
   653	def revert_loop_quarantine(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   654	    """§5.1 #5 — reverted > 5× in 24h: add ``coord-quarantine`` + @operator."""
   655	    t = ctx.ticket
   656	    if t is None:
   657	        return None
   658	    if t.revert_count_24h <= REVERT_LOOP_QUARANTINE_THRESHOLD:
   659	        return None
   660	    if has_label(t, COORD_QUARANTINE_LABEL):
   661	        return None  # already quarantined — don't re-fire each tick
   662	    return Action.relabel(
   663	        t.key,
   664	        add=(COORD_QUARANTINE_LABEL, NEEDS_OPERATOR_ACTION_LABEL),
   665	    )
   666	
   667	
   668	@rule(name="merger-repeat-fail", priority=20)
   669	def merger_repeat_fail(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   670	    """§5.1 #4 — merger failed > 3× on one ticket: escalate with context."""
   671	    t = ctx.ticket
   672	    if t is None:
   673	        return None
   674	    if t.merger_fail_count <= MERGER_FAIL_ESCALATE_THRESHOLD:
   675	        return None
   676	    if has_label(t, NEEDS_OPERATOR_ACTION_LABEL):
   677	        return None  # already escalated
   678	    return Action.escalate(
   679	        t.key,
   680	        reason=(
   681	            f"[merger-repeat-fail] merger failed {t.merger_fail_count} times on "
   682	            f"{t.key}; conflict is not auto-resolvable — operator merge needed."
   683	        ),
   684	    )
   685	
   686	
   687	@rule(name="stuck-in-progress", priority=30)
   688	def stuck_in_progress(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   689	    """§5.1 #7 — 進行中 > 2× CLI timeout, no live runner: reconcile to To Do.
   690	
   691	    The single-ticket equivalent of Phase Startup-2's "no commits, no recent
   692	    activity → restart cleanly": revert to To Do + clear the claim so a fresh
   693	    runner can re-pick it. Abstains when a runner is alive (it's handling) or
   694	    the ticket isn't actually stale.
   695	    """
   696	    t = ctx.ticket
   697	    if t is None or t.status not in IN_PROGRESS_STATUS_NAMES:
   698	        return None
   699	    if t.has_live_runner:
   700	        return None
   701	    age = _age_seconds(ctx.now, t.in_progress_since)
   702	    if age is None or age <= _stale_threshold_seconds():
   703	        return None
   704	    return Action.transition(t.key, to_status="To Do")
   705	
   706	
   707	@rule(name="stale-claim-cleanup", priority=40)
   708	def stale_claim_cleanup(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   709	    """§5.1 #2 — ``claim:default:*`` > 2× CLI timeout, no live runner: clear.
   710	
   711	    Removes the orphaned claim label(s) (ADR §3.3 Startup-3 stale-state sweep
   712	    for a single ticket). Assignee clearing rides along in the action layer's
   713	    relabel handler. Abstains while a runner is alive or the claim is fresh.
   714	    """
   715	    t = ctx.ticket
   716	    if t is None or t.has_live_runner:
   717	        return None
   718	    claim_labels = labels_with_prefix(t, CLAIM_LABEL_PREFIX)
   719	    if not claim_labels:
   720	        return None
   721	    age = _age_seconds(ctx.now, t.claim_started_at)
   722	    if age is None or age <= _stale_threshold_seconds():
   723	        return None
   724	    return Action.relabel(t.key, remove=claim_labels)
   725	
   726	
   727	@rule(name="runner-blocked-marker-stale", priority=50)
   728	def runner_blocked_marker_stale(ctx: DecisionContext) -> "Action | None":
   729	    """§5.1 #3 — ``runner-blocked:waiting-X`` where X is 公開済み: drop marker.
   730	
   731	    Resolves each ``runner-blocked:waiting-<KEY>`` against ``ctx.tickets``;
   732	    any blocker already Published is a dead wait — remove that marker so the
   733	    blocked ticket is pickable again. Abstains if blockers are unknown or
   734	    still open.
   735	    """

 succeeded in 0ms:
   140	                capacity_path_from_env(self.config_dir),
   141	            )
   142	        if self.bridge_events_path is None:
   143	            object.__setattr__(
   144	                self,
   145	                "bridge_events_path",
   146	                self.config_dir / "bridge-events.jsonl",
   147	            )
   148	
   149	    @classmethod
   150	    def from_env(
   151	        cls,
   152	        env: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   153	        *,
   154	        config_dir: Path | None = None,
   155	    ) -> "CoordinatorConfig":
   156	        env = env if env is not None else dict(os.environ)
   157	        base = (
   158	            Path(config_dir)
   159	            if config_dir is not None
   160	            else Path(env.get(CONFIG_DIR_ENV, str(DEFAULT_CONFIG_DIR))).expanduser()
   161	        )
   162	        decision_log_dir = Path(
   163	            env.get(DECISION_LOG_DIR_ENV, str(base / "decision-log"))
   164	        ).expanduser()
   165	        return cls(
   166	            config_dir=base,
   167	            heartbeat_path=base / "heartbeat",
   168	            decision_log_dir=decision_log_dir,
   169	            capacity_path=capacity_path_from_env(base, env),
   170	            bridge_events_path=Path(
   171	                env.get(BRIDGE_EVENTS_FILE_ENV, str(base / "bridge-events.jsonl"))
   172	            ).expanduser(),
   173	            jira_agent_class=env.get(JIRA_AGENT_CLASS_ENV, "subscription-claude"),
   174	        )
   175	
   260	        return None

   263	# ── Action layer (ADR-0021 §6) — shadow stub for 29f-3 ────────────────
   264	
   265	
   266	class ShadowActionExecutor:
   267	    """Records actions instead of executing them (ADR §6 / shadow mode).
   268	
   269	    The real §6 action layer (JIRA mutate / @-operator) lands in a later
   270	    phase; until then — and during the 29f-14 7-day shadow canary — the
   271	    coordinator must *observe* what a rule would do without touching JIRA.
   272	    This executor returns a per-action result record (``executed=False``)
   273	    that the daemon folds into the decision-log line, so the integration AC
   274	    ("rule returns Action → action layer executes it (mock if not yet built)
   275	    → action recorded in decision log") is satisfied without any mutation.
   276	
   277	    A real executor is a drop-in: same ``execute`` signature, returning
   278	    ``executed=True`` plus the mutation outcome.
   279	    """
   280	
   281	    #: Stamped into each result so a decision-log reader can tell shadow
   282	    #: (observed-only) executions from real ones.
   283	    executed: bool = False
   284	
   285	    def execute(self, action: Action, ctx: DecisionContext) -> dict[str, Any]:
   286	        return {
   287	            "kind": action.kind,
   288	            "target": action.target,
   289	            "executed": self.executed,
   290	            "shadow": True,
   291	        }
   292	
   293	
   294	# Action executor seam: anything with ``execute(action, ctx) -> dict``.
   295	ActionExecutor = Callable[[Action, DecisionContext], dict]
   296	
   297	
   298	# ── Event sources (ADR-0021 §4) ──────────────────────────────────────
   299	
   300	
   301	class EventDeduper:
   302	    """Five-minute coalescing window for identical source events."""
   303	
   304	    def __init__(
   305	        self,
   606	class JiraDispatchColdStartGateway:
   607	    """Production :class:`ColdStartGateway` over ``jira_dispatch`` + git + shell.
   608	
   609	    Heavy collaborators (``jira_dispatch.make_client``, the Gerrit SSH path)
   610	    are imported + constructed lazily and wrapped so a failure degrades to a
   611	    safe default — the daemon must reach its loop even on a half-up host.
   612	    The agent class used for the JIRA/Gerrit identity defaults to the
   613	    coordinator's own bot account (``claude``); it only ever *reads* + applies
   614	    the §6.1-bounded actions (relabel / transition / comment), never +2.
   615	    """
   616	
   617	    def __init__(
   618	        self,
   619	        *,
   620	        repo_root: Path | None = None,
   621	        agent_class: str = "claude",
   622	        cli_timeout_seconds: float = 60.0,
   623	    ) -> None:
   624	        self._repo_root = Path(repo_root) if repo_root is not None else _repo_root()
   625	        self._agent_class = agent_class
   626	        self._cli_timeout = cli_timeout_seconds
   627	        self._client: Any = None
   628	        self._client_tried = False
   629	
   630	    # ── lazy JIRA client (fail-open) ──
   631	    def _jira(self) -> Any:
   632	        if self._client is None and not self._client_tried:
   633	            self._client_tried = True
   634	            try:
   635	                from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   636	
   637	                self._client = jira_dispatch.make_client(self._agent_class)
   638	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — degrade, never wedge boot
   639	                logger.warning(
   640	                    "[pipeline_coordinator] cold-start JIRA client unavailable "
   641	                    "(reconcile/sweep degraded): %s",
   642	                    exc,
   643	                )
   644	                self._client = None
   645	        return self._client
   646	
   647	    # ── Startup-1 ──
   648	    def audit_infra(self) -> InfraAuditResult:
   649	        return run_deployment_audit(
   650	            repo_root=self._repo_root, timeout_seconds=self._cli_timeout
   651	        )
   652	
   653	    def start_unit(self, unit: str) -> bool:
   654	        try:
   655	            proc = subprocess.run(
   656	                ["systemctl", "--user", "start", unit],
   657	                capture_output=True,
   658	                text=True,
   659	                timeout=self._cli_timeout,
   660	            )
   661	        except (OSError, subprocess.SubprocessError) as exc:
   662	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] systemctl start %s failed: %s", unit, exc)
   663	            return False
   664	        if proc.returncode != 0:
   665	            logger.warning(
   666	                "[pipeline_coordinator] systemctl start %s rc=%d: %s",
   667	                unit, proc.returncode, proc.stderr.strip(),
   668	            )
   669	        return proc.returncode == 0
   670	
   671	    # ── Startup-2 ──
   672	    def interrupted_tickets(self) -> list[InterruptedTicket]:
   673	        client = self._jira()
   674	        if client is None:
   675	            return []
   676	        try:
   677	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   678	
   679	            jql = (
   680	                f'project = "{client.project_key}" '
   681	                'AND status in ("In Progress", "進行中") '
   682	                "ORDER BY updated ASC"
   683	            )
   684	            resp = jira_dispatch._request(
   685	                client, "POST", "/search/jql",
   686	                {"jql": jql, "fields": ["status", "updated"], "maxResults": 100},
   687	            )
   688	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   689	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] interrupted-ticket query failed: %s", exc)
   690	            return []
   691	        from backend.agents.pipeline_coordinator_rules import (
   692	            DEFAULT_CLI_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
   693	            STALE_MULTIPLIER,
   694	        )
   695	
   696	        stale_after = DEFAULT_CLI_TIMEOUT_SECONDS * STALE_MULTIPLIER
   697	        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   698	        out: list[InterruptedTicket] = []
   699	        for issue in resp.get("issues", []):
   700	            fields = issue.get("fields") or {}
   701	            status = str((fields.get("status") or {}).get("name", ""))
   702	            # ``updated`` is a coarse last-activity proxy for staleness (the
   703	            # exact 進行中-entry time needs the changelog; updated is a safe,
   704	            # cheaper lower bound — if it's old, the ticket is certainly stale).
   705	            stale = False
   706	            updated_raw = fields.get("updated")
   707	            if isinstance(updated_raw, str):
   708	                try:
   709	                    updated = datetime.fromisoformat(updated_raw.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
   710	                    stale = (now - updated).total_seconds() > stale_after
   711	                except ValueError:
   712	                    pass
   713	            out.append(
   714	                InterruptedTicket(key=issue.get("key", "?"), status=status, stale=stale)
   715	            )
   716	        return out
   717	
   718	    def has_live_runner(self, key: str) -> bool:
   719	        return _ticket_has_live_runner(key, repo_root=self._repo_root)
   720	
   721	    def gerrit_change_mergeable(self, key: str) -> bool:
   722	        # Conservative: only a *positively confirmed* mergeable open change
   723	        # routes a ticket to Under Review (§3.3 (b)). When the Gerrit query
   724	        # is unavailable we return False so the ticket falls through to the
   725	        # resume / revert / operator branches rather than a wrong transition.
   726	        try:
   727	            return _gerrit_open_change_mergeable(key, self._agent_class)
   728	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   729	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] gerrit mergeable check failed for %s: %s", key, exc)
   730	            return False
   731	
   732	    def branch_has_commits(self, key: str) -> bool:
   733	        return _feature_branch_has_commits(key, repo_root=self._repo_root)
   734	
   735	    def mark_resumable(self, key: str) -> None:
   736	        self._add_label(key, RESUME_FROM_FEATURE_LABEL)
   737	
   738	    def transition_under_review(self, key: str) -> None:
   739	        client = self._jira()
   740	        if client is None:
   741	            return
   742	        try:
   743	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   744	
   745	            # Idempotent per OP-691: skips when already Under Review.
   746	            jira_dispatch.transition_to_under_review_if_needed(client, key)
   747	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   748	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] transition_under_review(%s) failed: %s", key, exc)
   749	
   750	    def reset_to_todo(self, key: str) -> None:
   751	        client = self._jira()
   752	        if client is None:
   753	            return
   754	        try:
   755	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   756	
   757	            jira_dispatch.transition_back_to_todo(
   758	                client, key,
   759	                reason="[cold-start Startup-2(c)] interrupted with no commits/activity; "
   760	                       "reverting to To Do for clean re-pickup.",
   761	            )
   762	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   763	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] reset_to_todo(%s) failed: %s", key, exc)
   764	
   765	    # ── Startup-3 ──
   766	    def stale_sweep_plan(self) -> StaleSweepPlan:
   767	        client = self._jira()
   768	        if client is None:
   769	            return StaleSweepPlan()
   770	        try:
   771	            return _build_stale_sweep_plan(client, repo_root=self._repo_root)
   772	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   773	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] stale-sweep plan failed: %s", exc)
   774	            return StaleSweepPlan()
   775

   779	            return
   780	        try:
   781	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   782	
   783	            jira_dispatch.remove_label(client, key, label)
   784	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   785	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] remove_label(%s, %s) failed: %s", key, label, exc)
   786	
   787	    def clear_assignee(self, key: str) -> None:
   788	        client = self._jira()
   789	        if client is None:
   790	            return
   791	        try:
   792	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   793	
   794	            jira_dispatch.clear_assignee(client, key)
   795	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   796	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] clear_assignee(%s) failed: %s", key, exc)
   797	
   798	    # ── shared ──
   799	    def mention_operator(self, key: str, message: str, *, urgency: str = "high") -> None:
   800	        client = self._jira()
   801	        if client is None:
   802	            logger.error("[pipeline_coordinator] OPERATOR-ALERT (JIRA down) %s: %s", key, message)
   803	            return
   804	        try:
   805	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   806	
   807	            jira_dispatch.add_comment(client, key, f"@nanakusa-sora {message}")
   808	            if urgency == "high":
   809	                jira_dispatch.add_label(client, key, "needs-operator-action")
   810	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
   811	            logger.warning("[pipeline_coordinator] mention_operator(%s) failed: %s", key, exc)
   812	
   813	    def _add_label(self, key: str, label: str) -> None:
   814	        client = self._jira()
   815	        if client is None:
   816	            return
   817	        try:
   818	            from backend.agents import jira_dispatch
   819	
   820	            jira_dispatch.add_label(client, key, label)
  1140	    """Production gateway wiring (no I/O at construction — lazy + fail-open).
  1141	
  1142	    ``acting=False`` (the default — the 29f-14 7-day shadow canary, OP-1555)
  1143	    wraps the live gateway in :class:`ShadowColdStartGateway` so cold-start
  1144	    observes the world but performs zero JIRA/git/systemctl mutation;
  1145	    ``acting=True`` returns the live gateway so the 4-phase recovery mutates as
  1146	    designed. The same single ``acting`` switch gates the engine action layer
  1147	    and the sprint re-plan handler (see :func:`build_default_coordinator`).
  1148	    """
  1149	    live = JiraDispatchColdStartGateway()
  1150	    return live if acting else ShadowColdStartGateway(live)
  1151

  1153	# ── Daemon ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1154	
  1155	
  1156	class PipelineCoordinator:
  1157	    """Skeleton coordinator daemon: tick → evaluate → log no-op → heartbeat.
  1158	
  1510	            }
  1511	            report.crash_recoveries.append(entry)
  1512	            self._log_cold_start(entry)
  1513	
  1514	    def _resolve_interrupted_actions(self, tick: dict[str, Any] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  1515	        """Map each interrupted action of a crashed tick to a recovery step."""
  1516	        if not tick:
  1517	            return [{"resolution": "noop", "reason": "no in-flight real action before crash"}]
  1518	        gw = self._cold_start_gateway
  1519	        out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  1520	        for action in tick.get("actions", []):
  1521	            kind = action.get("kind")
  1522	            target = action.get("target", "")
  1523	            if kind in (ACTION_RELABEL, ACTION_TRANSITION):
  1524	                # Idempotent → safe to complete (re-issue) on recovery.
  1525	                if kind == ACTION_TRANSITION and action.get("params", {}).get(
  1526	                    "to_status"
  1527	                ) == "Under Review":
  1528	                    gw.transition_under_review(target)
  1529	                resolution = "completed"
  1530	            elif kind == ACTION_FILE_TICKET:
  1531	                # Not safely reversible/repeatable — surface to operator.
  1532	                gw.mention_operator(
  1533	                    target,
  1534	                    f"[cold-start L6] crash interrupted a file_ticket action on {target}; "
  1535	                    "verify the dependency ticket exists and was not double-filed.",
  1536	                    urgency="medium",
  1537	                )
  1538	                resolution = "escalated"
  1539	            else:
  1540	                resolution = "noop"
  1541	            out.append({"kind": kind, "target": target, "resolution": resolution})
  1542	        return out
  1688	        """One tick: heartbeat → refresh world → evaluate → log decision.
  1689	
  1690	        Returns the engine's :class:`DecisionResult` (a no-op in the
  1691	        skeleton) so callers/tests can assert on it directly.
  1692	        """
  1693	        self._heartbeat.touch()
  1694	        self._refresh_work_graph()
  1695	        self._append_source_event_records()
  1696	        ctx = self.build_context()
  1697	        if self._config.capacity_path is not None:
  1698	            write_capacity_snapshot(ctx.capacity, self._config.capacity_path)
  1699	        result = self._engine.evaluate(ctx)
  1700	        # Hand each real (non-noop) action to the action layer. In shadow
  1701	        # mode it only records the would-be execution; the outcomes go into
  1702	        # the decision-log line so a fired rule is observable end-to-end.
  1703	        action_results = [
  1704	            self._action_executor(a, ctx)
  1705	            for a in result.actions
  1706	            if not isinstance(a, NoopAction)
  1707	        ]
  1708	        record = self._build_tick_record(result, action_results)
  1709	        self._decision_log.append(record)
  1710	        self._run_learning_loop(record, result)
  1711	        return result
  1712	
  1862	def build_default_coordinator(
  1863	    config: CoordinatorConfig | None = None,
  1864	    *,
  1865	    acting: bool = False,
  1866	    action_executor: ActionExecutor | None = None,
  1867	) -> PipelineCoordinator:
  1868	    """Production wiring: env config, hybrid Tier-1+Tier-2 engine, shadow layer.
  1869	
  1870	    29f-3 shipped the Tier-1 rule engine; 29f-6 fronts it with the Tier-2 LLM
  1871	    consultant (:class:`HybridDecisionEngine`) so a tick that no Tier-1 rule
  1872	    matches (or that the personality mode escalates) consults the claude CLI
  1873	    under a daily budget cap. Constructing the engine logs the loaded rule
  1874	    registry (Deploy AC). The budget guard rebuilds the day's spend from the
  1875	    decision log (ADR §3.2 stateless-across-restarts). The action layer
  1876	    defaults to shadow (observe-only) so this stays safe for the 29f-14 7-day
  1877	    shadow canary. ``acting=True`` is the single switch that permits the main
  1878	    action executor, the hourly sprint re-plan handler, AND the 4-phase
  1879	    cold-start recovery to mutate; ``acting=False`` wires every one of them
  1880	    observe-only (cold-start via :class:`ShadowColdStartGateway`, OP-1555).
  1881	    """
  1882	    config = config or CoordinatorConfig.from_env()
  1883	    if acting and action_executor is None:
  1884	        raise ValueError("acting=True requires an explicit action_executor")
  1885	    if not acting and action_executor is not None:
  1886	        raise ValueError("action_executor requires acting=True")
  1887	    decision_log = DecisionLog(config.decision_log_dir)
  1888	    # 29f-11: the learning loop reads + appends to the same decision log; build
  1889	    # it from the log so its daily/weekly schedule survives a restart (§3.2).
  1890	    from backend.agents.learning_loop import build_default_learning_loop
  1891	
  1892	    return PipelineCoordinator(
  1893	        config,
  1894	        engine=build_hybrid_engine(decision_log_dir=config.decision_log_dir),
  1895	        action_executor=action_executor,
  1896	        cold_start_gateway=_default_cold_start_gateway(config, acting=acting),
  1897	        sprint_replan_handler=build_default_sprint_replan_handler(
  1898	            config_dir=config.config_dir,
  1899	            decision_log_dir=config.decision_log_dir,
  1900	            capacity_path=config.capacity_path,
  1901	            agent_class=config.jira_agent_class,
  1902	            shadow=not acting,
  1903	        ),
  1904	        decision_log=decision_log,
  1905	        learning_loop=build_default_learning_loop(decision_log),
  1906	    )
  1907	
  1908	
  1909	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
  1910	    """CLI entrypoint. ``--once`` runs a single tick; default runs forever."""
  1911	    import argparse
  1912	
  1913	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
  1914	    parser.add_argument(
  1915	        "--once",
  1916	        action="store_true",
  1917	        help="run a single tick (heartbeat + one no-op decision) and exit "
  1918	             "(skips cold-start recovery — a diagnostic tick, not a boot)",
  1919	    )
  1920	    parser.add_argument(
  1921	        "--no-cold-start",
  1922	        action="store_true",
  1923	        help="enter the steady-state loop directly, skipping the 4-phase "
  1924	             "cold-start recovery (ADR §3.3) — for local debugging only",
  1925	    )
  1926	    parser.add_argument(
  1927	        "--config-dir",
  1928	        type=Path,
  1929	        default=None,
  1930	        help="override the coordinator config dir (default ~/.config/omnisight/coordinator)",
  1931	    )
  1932	    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
  1933	
  1934	    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)
  1935	    config = CoordinatorConfig.from_env(config_dir=args.config_dir)
  1936	    coordinator = build_default_coordinator(config)
  1937	    if args.once:
  1938	        coordinator.run_once()
  1939	        return 0
  1940	    if args.no_cold_start:
  1941	        coordinator.run_forever()
  1942	        return 0

  1650	    client: "DispatchClient",
  1651	    key: str,
  1652	    idem_key: str | None = None,
  1653	    change_number: int | None = None,
  1654	) -> bool:
  1655	    """In Progress → Under Review, but only if not already there.
  1656	
  1657	    Returns True if a transition POST was issued, False if the ticket was
  1658	    already in Under Review and the call was a no-op. Raises RuntimeError
  1659	    on any non-4xx HTTP failure of the transition POST itself; the runner

  1670	        client, "POST", f"/issue/{key}/transitions",
  1671	        {"transition": {"id": TRANSITION_IDS["to_under_review"]}},
  1672	        idem_key,
  1673	    )
  1674	    return True
  1934	def transition_back_to_todo(
  1935	    client: DispatchClient,
  1936	    key: str,
  1937	    reason: str,
  1938	    idem_key: str | None = None,
  1939	    *,
  1940	    failure_class: "str | None" = None,
  1941	    raw_traceback: str = "",
  1942	    mutex_label: str | None = None,
  1943	    area: str | None = None,
  1944	) -> None:
  1945	    """In Progress → TODO with reason comment + clear assignee.
  1946	
  1947	    OP-1541 invariant: this is a destructive recovery — it clears the
  1948	    assignee. Under a shared JIRA account ONLY the current winning
  1949	    fencing-token claim holder may revert. Callers on the TOCTOU /
  1950	    assignee-divergence path MUST first prove claim ownership (see
  1951	    :func:`is_winning_claim_owner` and ``auto-runner-jira._handle_toctou_abort``)
  1952	    so a sibling instance's assignee clear does not cascade into a false
  1953	    revert (OP-1533 burst → stoploss). The non-recovery callers (CLI
  1954	    failure, capability gate, discovered dependency, pre-claim cwd-unsafe)
  1955	    are unaffected — they own or predate the claim.
  1956	
  1957	    OP-854 (C2): when ``failure_class`` is supplied, an incident row
  1958	    lands in ``runner_incidents`` before the transition fires so the
  1959	    C8 failure-graph and the C2 recall path can pick it up next time
  1960	    the ticket is attempted. Recording is best-effort — a recorder
  1961	    fault never blocks the JIRA transition (the operator still needs
  1962	    the ticket reverted even if the audit shim is wedged).
  1963	    """
  1964	    base_key = idem_key or f"transition-{key}-back-to-todo-{uuid.uuid4().hex[:12]}"
  1965	    if failure_class is not None:
  1966	        try:
  1967	            # Local import keeps jira_dispatch importable without the
  1968	            # incident_recorder + failure_class chain (e.g. early-boot
  1969	            # JQL-only smoke tests).
  1970	            from backend.agents.incident_recorder import record_runner_incident
  1971	
  1972	            record_runner_incident(
  1973	                ticket_key=key,
  1974	                failure_class=failure_class,
  1975	                summary=reason.splitlines()[0] if reason else "",
  1976	                raw_traceback=raw_traceback,
  1977	                runner_class=getattr(client, "agent_class", "unknown"),
  1978	                mutex_label=mutex_label,
  1979	                area=area,
  1980	            )
  1981	        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — best-effort wiring
  1982	            log.warning(
  1983	                "jira_dispatch.record_runner_incident_failed key=%s err=%s",
  1984	                key,
  1985	                exc,
  1986	            )
  1987	    add_comment(
  1988	        client, key, f"Reverting to TODO. Reason:\n{reason}",
  1989	        idem_key=f"{base_key}-comment",
  1990	    )
  1991	    clear_assignee(client, key, idem_key=f"{base_key}-clear-assignee")
  1992	    _request_idempotent(
  1993	        client, "POST", f"/issue/{key}/transitions",
  1994	        {"transition": {"id": TRANSITION_IDS["back_to_todo"]}},
  1995	        f"{base_key}-transition",
  1996	    )
  1997	
  1998	    # OP-1140: stoploss circuit-breaker recording — best-effort.
  1999	    # A recorder fault (network blip, label-fetch 404 on a freshly-created
  2000	    # ticket, ...) must NEVER prevent the §11-revert from completing; the
  2001	    # operator still needs the ticket re-armed for triage even if the
  2002	    # counter slips one tick.
  2003	    try:
  2004	        from backend.agents import runner_stoploss
  2005	
  2006	        labels_now = fetch_labels(client, key)
  2007	        runner_stoploss.register_revert(client, key, labels_now)
  2008	    except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 — best-effort wiring
  2009	        log.warning(
  2010	            "jira_dispatch.register_revert_failed key=%s err=%s", key, exc,
  2011	        )

    90	    def run(self, key: str, fn: Callable[[], Any]) -> Any:
    91	        self.prune()
    92	        cached = self.get(key)
    93	        if cached is not None:
    94	            return cached
    95	        response = fn()
    96	        self.put(key, response if response is not None else {})
    97	        return response

/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba docs/operations/coordinator-runbook.md | sed -n '20,60p;127,145p'" in /home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer
 succeeded in 0ms:
    20	The coordinator is a single long-running **user** systemd daemon
    21	(`backend.agents.pipeline_coordinator`) that, every tick: writes a
    22	heartbeat, drains its event sources (bridge tap + 60 s JIRA poll +
    23	hourly sweep), runs the hybrid decision engine (Tier-1 rules →
    24	Tier-2 LLM consult), and appends one JSON line per decision to the
    25	append-only decision log.
    26	
    27	Two facts dominate every operator decision below:
    28	
    29	1. **The steady-state action layer is `ShadowActionExecutor` by
    30	   default** (`build_default_coordinator`,
    31	   `backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1709`). It *records* what a
    32	   rule would do (`executed=false`, `shadow=true`) but **does not mutate
    33	   JIRA**. This is the safe posture for the 29f-14 7-day shadow canary.
    34	   Acting mode is enabled later by swapping the executor — until then,
    35	   a "scary" decision-log line is an *observation*, not an action.
    36	2. **Cold-start recovery DOES act.** The 4-phase boot recovery
    37	   (ADR §3.3 / `pipeline_coordinator.py:475+`) transitions tickets,
    38	   relabels, and clears assignees for real on every daemon boot. So a
    39	   restart is not side-effect-free — see [§5](#5-pause--stop) before
    40	   restarting during an incident.
    41	
    42	| Concept | Where it lives |
    43	|---|---|
    44	| Daemon unit | `pipeline-coordinator.service` (user) |
    45	| Watchdog unit | `pipeline-coordinator-watchdog.service` (user) |
    46	| Working dir | `/home/user/sora-bridge` (auto-synced to `develop`) |
    47	| Config dir | `/home/user/.config/omnisight/coordinator/` |
    48	| Heartbeat | `…/coordinator/heartbeat` (rewritten every 60 s) |
    49	| Decision log | `…/coordinator/decision-log/YYYY-MM-DD.jsonl` (0600) |
    50	| Daemon stdout/stderr | `/home/user/work/sora/logs/coordinator/systemd.log` |
    51	| Watchdog logs | journal, `SyslogIdentifier=pipeline-coordinator-watchdog` |
    52	
    53	> The deployed units set their environment **inline** via
    54	> `Environment=` directives — there is **no `EnvironmentFile`**. The
    55	> `budget.env` path mentioned in ADR-0021 §5.3 is **not wired** by the
    56	> shipped unit; set budget/model knobs with a systemd drop-in instead
    57	> ([§3.1](#31-changing-an-env-knob)).
    58	
    59	## 1. Monitoring
    60	
   127	### Coordinator daemon — `pipeline_coordinator.py`
   128	
   129	| Env var | Default | Meaning |
   130	|---|---|---|
   131	| `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_CONFIG_DIR` | `~/.config/omnisight/coordinator` | Base config dir (heartbeat, decision-log, capacity, bridge-events live under it). |
   132	| `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_DECISION_LOG_DIR` | `<config_dir>/decision-log` | Append-only decision log dir (one `YYYY-MM-DD.jsonl` per UTC day). Shared with the bridge so both write one audit trail. |
   133	| `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_BRIDGE_EVENTS_FILE` | `<config_dir>/bridge-events.jsonl` | Bridge event tap the coordinator tails (§4 source). |
   134	| `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_JIRA_AGENT_CLASS` | `subscription-claude` | JIRA client class used for the 60 s poll + mutations. |
   135	| `OMNISIGHT_RUNNER_CAPACITY_PATH` | `<config_dir>/runner_capacity.json` | Capacity summary the coordinator maintains from runner quota telemetry (`pipeline_coordinator_capacity.py:33`). |
   136	
   137	### Tier-2 LLM consultation — `pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py`
   138	
   139	| Env var | Default | Meaning |
   140	|---|---|---|
   141	| `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_DAILY_BUDGET_USD` | `5.0` | Per-UTC-day Tier-2 spend cap. **`<= 0` disables Tier-2 entirely** — the operator kill switch for LLM spend. On cap-hit the daemon degrades to Tier-1-only and alerts (reason `tier2_degraded_budget_cap`). |
   142	| `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_LLM_MODEL` | `claude-opus-4-7` | Model passed to the claude CLI. |
   143	| `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_LLM_TIMEOUT_S` | `90.0` | Per-consult CLI timeout (seconds). |
   144	| `OMNISIGHT_COORDINATOR_CLAUDE_CLI` | `claude` | Path/name of the claude CLI binary. Must be on the daemon's `PATH`. |

   261	
   262	@dataclass(frozen=True)
   263	class DecisionResult:
   264	    """Outcome of one ``evaluate`` call.
   265	
   266	    ``decision_id`` is a fresh UUID per tick so a decision-log line can be
   267	    correlated with downstream action records (and L6 crash-recovery can
   268	    pair ``shutdown_began`` / ``shutdown_complete`` with the right tick).
   269	    """
   270	
   271	    decision_id: str
   272	    actions: tuple[Action, ...]
   273	    reason: str
   274	    mode: str
   275	    engine_version: str
   276	    # The selected mode's behavior overlay (ADR-0021 §7.2), or ``None`` for
   277	    # an idle/skeleton tick. Tier-1 rules + Tier-2 LLM read this to know how
   278	    # hard to lean on rules vs. LLM, how wide to make the LLM context, and
   279	    # whether to file a follow-up — the Integration-AC "see + respect the
   280	    # selected mode" seam.
   281	    mode_behavior: PersonalityMode | None = None
   282	    # 29f-3: which Tier-1 rule produced this result (ADR Appendix C
   283	    # ``rule_name``) and which tier decided. Default ``None``/``None`` keeps
   284	    # the skeleton engine's construction (which names neither) valid.
   285	    rule_name: str | None = None
   286	    tier: int | None = None
   287	    # 29f-6: the Tier-2 LLM-consultation metadata block (ADR Appendix C
   288	    # ``llm_consultation`` — context/response token counts, cost, confidence,
   289	    # decision rationale, cited lessons, or a budget-degrade marker). ``None``
   290	    # for a Tier-1 / skeleton tick that never consulted the LLM.
   291	    llm_consultation: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
   292	
   293	    @property
   294	    def is_noop(self) -> bool:
   295	        return all(isinstance(a, NoopAction) for a in self.actions) or not self.actions
   296	
   297	
   298	@runtime_checkable
   299	class DecisionEngineProtocol(Protocol):
   300	    """The seam 29f-3 implements; the daemon depends on this, not the class."""
   301	
   302	    engine_version: str
   303	
   304	    def evaluate(self, ctx: DecisionContext) -> DecisionResult: ...
   305	
   306	
   307	class DecisionEngine:
   308	    """Skeleton decision engine — selects a mode, then logs one no-op per tick.
   309	
   310	    Real Tier-1 rules + Tier-2 LLM consultation land in 29f-3 / 29f-6. What
   311	    AUDIT-29f-5 wires here is the **mode-selection-before-rule-evaluation**
   312	    contract (ADR-0021 §7, Integration AC): when a tick carries a situation
   313	    profile, the engine asks its :class:`ModeSelector` for the personality
   314	    mode *before* any rule body would run, and threads the selected mode +
   315	    its behavior overlay through the result so the (future) Tier-1 rules and
   316	    Tier-2 LLM consultation can read + respect it. An idle tick (no
   317	    ``situation``) keeps the skeleton ``mode`` so a no-op still logs "skeleton".
   318	
   319	    Keeping the skeleton a concrete class (rather than only a Protocol) lets
   320	    the daemon default-construct an engine and run end-to-end today.
   321	    """
   322	
   323	    engine_version: str = SKELETON_ENGINE_VERSION
   324	
   325	    def __init__(self, *, mode_selector: ModeSelector | None = None) -> None:
   326	        self._mode_selector = mode_selector or ModeSelector()
   327	
   328	    def evaluate(self, ctx: DecisionContext) -> DecisionResult:
   329	        """Select the mode, then return a single :class:`NoopAction`.
   330	
   331	        Mode selection runs first (before any rule body) and is the only
   332	        thing this skeleton engine decides; the action set stays a no-op
   333	        until 29f-3 lands the Tier-1 rules. No I/O.
   334	        """
   335	        mode = ctx.mode
   336	        if ctx.situation is not None:
   337	            mode = self._mode_selector.select(ctx.situation, override=ctx.mode_override)
   338	        behavior = mode_behavior(mode)
   339	        # (29f-3 Tier-1 rules + 29f-6 Tier-2 LLM consultation read `behavior`
   340	        #  here to steer rule-vs-LLM weighting, LLM context width, follow-up
   341	        #  filing — then populate `actions`. The skeleton stays no-op.)
   342	        return DecisionResult(
   343	            decision_id=uuid.uuid4().hex,
   344	            actions=(NoopAction(),),
   345	            reason=SKELETON_NOOP_REASON,
   346	            mode=mode,
   347	            engine_version=self.engine_version,
   348	            mode_behavior=behavior,
   888	            ", ".join(f"{r.name}@{r.priority}" for r in self._rules),
   889	        )
   890	
   891	    @property
   892	    def rules(self) -> tuple[RegisteredRule, ...]:
   893	        return self._rules
   894	
   895	    def rule_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
   896	        return tuple(r.name for r in self._rules)
   897	
   898	    def evaluate(self, ctx: DecisionContext) -> DecisionResult:
   899	        """Iterate the registry in priority order; first Action wins."""
   900	        for r in self._rules:
   901	            action = r.evaluate(ctx)
   902	            if action is None:
   903	                continue
   904	            return DecisionResult(
   905	                decision_id=uuid.uuid4().hex,
   906	                actions=(action,),
   907	                reason=f"tier1:{r.name}",
   908	                mode=ctx.mode,
   909	                engine_version=self.engine_version,
   910	                rule_name=r.name,
   911	                tier=1,
   912	            )
   913	        # No Tier-1 rule matched → Tier-2 LLM consult territory (29f-6).
   914	        return DecisionResult(
   915	            decision_id=uuid.uuid4().hex,
   916	            actions=(NoopAction(),),
   917	            reason=TIER1_NO_MATCH_REASON,
   918	            mode=ctx.mode,
   919	            engine_version=self.engine_version,
   920	            rule_name=None,

  1092	    def _fired_rules(self, ctx: DecisionContext) -> list[tuple[RegisteredRule, Action]]:

   289	    """Tier-2 seam returning the operator escalation Scenario B expects."""
   290	
   291	    def consult(
   292	        self,
   293	        ctx: DecisionContext,
   294	        *,
   295	        behavior: Any = None,
   296	        trigger: str = "tier1_no_match",
   297	    ) -> Tier2Outcome:
   298	        assert ctx.ticket is not None
   299	        assert behavior is not None and behavior.requires_root_cause is True
   300	        assert ctx.capacity.runners["subscription-codex"].free_slots == 1
   301	        reason = (
   302	            "[merger-repeat-fail] OP-B merger failed 4 times; "
   303	            "full context: change Iabcdef, conflicts in src/a.py, "
   304	            "mode=RescueMode, free_codex_slots=1"
   305	        )
   306	        return Tier2Outcome(
   307	            actions=(Action.escalate(ctx.ticket.key, reason=reason),),
   308	            reason=TIER2_CONSULTED_REASON,
   309	            llm_consultation={
   310	                "trigger": trigger,
   311	                "confidence": "high",
   312	                "decision_rationale": reason,
   313	                "cost_usd": 0.01,
   314	                "lessons_cited": ["L-OP-870-blockedby.md"],
   315	            },
   316	        )
   317	
   318	
   319	def _config(tmp_path: Path) -> CoordinatorConfig:
   320	    base = tmp_path / "coordinator"
   321	    return CoordinatorConfig(
   322	        config_dir=base,
   323	        heartbeat_path=base / "heartbeat",
   324	        decision_log_dir=base / "decision-log",
   325	        heartbeat_interval_seconds=60.0,

**SECTION 1 — RE-AUDIT**

**BLOCKER**

- B1 — RESOLVED. v2 now dispatches on `action.kind`, matching the real closed vocabulary in [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:60) and the existing sprint pattern in [sprint_replan.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/sprint_replan.py:141). No v2 false claim here.

- B2 — RESOLVED. `Action.escalate()` means label + reason comment in [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:206), while gateway `mention_operator(..., urgency="critical")` would not add the label because it only labels on `"high"` in [pipeline_coordinator.py](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:807). v2 correctly makes escalate a distinct path.

- B3 — PARTIAL. Low-level helpers do accept `idem_key` today: `transition_to_under_review_if_needed` [jira_dispatch.py:1649](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:1649), `transition_back_to_todo` [jira_dispatch.py:1934](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:1934), comments/labels/assignee [jira_dispatch.py:2014](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:2014). But `coord:{decision_id}:...` does not dedupe repeated identical decisions because `decision_id` is fresh per tick [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:266](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:266), and `DEFAULT_STORE.run()` dedupes only exact key reuse [idempotency.py:90](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/idempotency.py:90). v2 changelog overstates this as fixed.

- B4 — PARTIAL. v2 moves the cap to `run_once`, the right place, because current execution is unbounded [pipeline_coordinator.py:1703](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1703). But the design leaves starvation and bulk-sweep semantics open; stale claim cleanup is one action per focal ticket [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:724](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:724), while cold-start sweeps still loop unbounded over plans [pipeline_coordinator.py:1410](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1410).

- B5 — RESOLVED. v2 no longer reuses the fail-open cold-start gateway, whose methods swallow exceptions and return `None` [pipeline_coordinator.py:746](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:746), [pipeline_coordinator.py:762](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:762). It correctly calls `jira_dispatch` helpers directly for real outcomes.

- B6 — PARTIAL. v2 recognizes that `acting=True` flips executor, sprint replan, and cold-start together [pipeline_coordinator.py:1895](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1895), [pipeline_coordinator.py:1902](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1902). But the boot-mutation cap is only sketched, not specified enough to implement safely across Startup-1 systemctl starts, L6, Startup-2, and Startup-3.

- S7 — RESOLVED in design. Current list comprehension lets executor exceptions abort before decision-log append [pipeline_coordinator.py:1703](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1703), [pipeline_coordinator.py:1708](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1708). v2’s per-action try/except fixes the right failure.

- S8 — RESOLVED in design, not implementation. `from_env` has no acting flag [pipeline_coordinator.py:149](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:149), and `main` still calls `build_default_coordinator(config)` shadow-only [pipeline_coordinator.py:1935](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1935). v2’s plan is correct.

But v2 does not specify which live read helper fetches labels or how failures fail closed.

**SECTION 2 — PLANNED-DIFF AUDIT**

**BLOCKER**

- LiveActionExecutor needs a stable client and label-read seam. `jira_dispatch.make_client(config.jira_agent_class)` is implied but not named; coord-skip recheck needs `fetch_labels()` or equivalent and must fail closed. Otherwise a JIRA label-read outage can mutate paused tickets. Evidence: low-level helpers require a `DispatchClient` [jira_dispatch.py:2014](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:2014), while `coord-skip` is an operator hard veto [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:606](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:606).

- `transition_back_to_todo` is destructive and clears assignee [jira_dispatch.py:1934](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:1934), [jira_dispatch.py:1991](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:1991). v2 maps steady-state stuck-in-progress directly to it, but does not require a fresh live-runner / ownership recheck immediately before mutation. The rule’s context may be stale by execution time [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:696](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:696).

- `dry_run` flip is under-specified and currently fatal to rollout. `run_once` logs `dry_run` from the action objects [pipeline_coordinator.py:1358](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1358), so simply setting config acting does not make decision ticks real.

**SHOULD-FIX**

- The per-tick cap will break or require updating tests that assume every action result appears. Current `run_once` executes all non-noop actions [pipeline_coordinator.py:1703](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1703). Add tests for capped `action_results`, and update integration assumptions around multi-action degraded outcomes [pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py:1115](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py:1115).

- Env wiring needs docs and service updates beyond code. The deployed unit has inline `Environment=` and no `EnvironmentFile` [pipeline-coordinator.service:29](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/deploy/systemd/pipeline-coordinator.service:29); the runbook explicitly says no `EnvironmentFile` [coordinator-runbook.md:53](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/docs/operations/coordinator-runbook.md:53). v2 activation’s `env.conf` wording is wrong for this repo.

Startup includes `systemctl start` [pipeline_coordinator.py:653](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:653), JIRA transitions/labels/comments [pipeline_coordinator.py:735](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:735), and sweep loops [pipeline_coordinator.py:776](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:776). **SECTION 3 — IMPLEMENTATION-RISK AUDIT**

**BLOCKER**

- Q7: No, nothing emits `dry_run=False` today. Acting mode will observe-only forever unless the engine rewrites actions or factories receive acting context. Evidence: defaults [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:126](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:126), Tier-1 pass-through [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:904](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:904), Tier-2 pass-through [pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py:1132](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_llm_consultation.py:1132).

- Q8: No, `coord:{decision_id}:...` does not dedupe across ticks. `decision_id` is fresh per tick [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:266](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:266); idempotency only caches exact key hits [idempotency.py:90](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/idempotency.py:90). Use `coord:{kind}:{target}:{content_hash}` plus subkeys per REST call.

- Q9: v2 still needs an explicit steady-state transition reason. The cold-start reason is wrong and hardcoded [pipeline_coordinator.py:757](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:757). For `stuck-in-progress`, use an operator-readable reason naming the rule, stale threshold, no-live-runner observation, and decision_id.

- Q11: v2 still misses crash windows before decision-log append. `run_once` executes actions before building/appending the tick record [pipeline_coordinator.py:1703](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1703), [pipeline_coordinator.py:1708](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator.py:1708). A process death after mutation but before append is invisible to L6.

**SHOULD-FIX**

- Q10: 1/tick is safe for first live flip but can starve legitimate sweeps. The current poll may surface many tickets, and rules like stale-claim-cleanup produce one relabel per ticket [pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:707](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/pipeline_coordinator_rules.py:707). Keep default 1, but design needs queue/fairness metrics and a documented ramp, not just an env knob.

- JIRA helper idem-key threading is not a prerequisite signature change for the named helpers; they already accept `idem_key`. The prerequisite is composite-key discipline: relabel with multiple labels must not reuse one idem key for multiple PUTs because `_request_idempotent` caches by key [jira_dispatch.py:331](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/backend/agents/jira_dispatch.py:331). Use suffixes like `:add:<label>`, `:remove:<label>`, `:clear-assignee`.

- Capability/systemd gap: `jira_update` exists in the runner capability vocabulary [capability_matrix.yaml:27](/home/user/work/sora/OmniSight-Productizer/config/capability_matrix.yaml:27), but the coordinator daemon itself is not capability-matrix gated. v2 should say this is a daemon env authorization, not runner pickup capability.

**NICE**

- `file_ticket` “not auto-executed + mention_operator” consumes one cap slot but performs a comment/label side effect. v2 should define whether this counts as executing `file_ticket`, `mention_operator`, or both in `action_results`, otherwise decision-log readers will misinterpret `executed:False` with side effects.

Verdict: needs-v3.
